// Symbols for overlay 3, game.bin: the player's states and the weapons, the
// monsters' common code and command scripts, the attached effects, the stage,
// area and ambient objects, the scene and message events.
// Format: name = 0xADDRESS; // attributes

// Motions: Em_SetMotion(em, row, ...) starts row `row` of the kind's motion

// Each frame: the distances and direction to each player (Em.playerDist on), the reaction ranges.

Em_SeePlayers = 0x00700A30; // type:func

// Curve-driven movement: eleven families move their monster along two curves
// (EmCurveKey tables sampled at a frame counter in the work area), the
// vertical velocity (Em.velY, which at the table's end falls by 1 a frame to
// -50, returning 2) and the forward speed (Em.velZ, 4 at the end). Each
// family's UpdateCurves runs the curves whose flags are set, advances the
// frame by the motion's step and returns the end flags; the first of its
// three slots is a stub that returns 0.

// The player's state machine: Player_RunState switches on Player.stateGroup;
// group 1 is the weapon's actions, Player_RunWeaponState, which switches on
// Player.weaponType (0 great sword, 1 heavy bowgun, 2 hammer, 3 lance, 4 sword
// and shield, 5 light bowgun, 6 dual swords, 7 long sword, 8 hunting horn, 9

// Monster status buildup: Em_AddStatusN(em, amount) adds to the status
// accumulator (unless Versus_TeamHasFlag says the monster is exempt) and sets
// its flag in Em.statusFlags when the kind's threshold is reached
// (EmStatusParam, gEmStatusNParams); Em_AddPoison the same for the poison
// (Em.poisonThreshold, Em.poisonBuildup, flag 4; its damage, gEmStatusParams
// +8, goes to Em.poisonDamage).
Em_AddStatus4 = 0x007251A0; // type:func
Em_AddStatus2 = 0x007252E0; // type:func
Em_AddStatus1 = 0x007253A0; // type:func
Em_AddStatus5 = 0x00725260; // type:func
Em_AddPoison = 0x007250B0; // type:func
// Em_UpdateStatus(em): the per-frame side. While poisoned (flag 4) it counts
// Em.poisonDamage down, spawns the poison effect (Effect06_SpawnAt, bone and
// offset per kind) every 60 frames and takes gEmStatusParams +6 from hp every
// +0xA frames (not below 1 when Em.guardTimer or Em.large); when the poison
// ends it raises the threshold by +0xC (at most 4 times, Em.poisonCount).
// Otherwise, and for the other statuses while their flag is clear, the buildup
// decays.
Em_UpdateStatus = 0x00724AB0; // type:func

// Turning: Em_StartTurn(em, work, back) sets the angle to the goal point
// (Em.goal, or the opposite way with back) in Em.aimAngle and starts the work
// area's motion: +0x10 with flag 0x20000 when the turn is within the tolerance
// +0xE, else the left (+0x14) or right (+0x18) turn.
Em_StartTurn = 0x0070B7D0; // type:func
Em_StartTurnToTarget = 0x0070B8E0; // type:func
Em_StartTurnAway = 0x0070B8F0; // type:func

// The monsters' command scripts: EmScript_Run interprets the monster's
// current script. It reads a command byte and calls the command's handler
// with the bytes after it, handler(em, p + 1), which returns the next command
// (at most 1000 commands a frame); commands 0x7B and 0x84 share
// EmScript_AdvanceStateRand. Many commands are conditionals: their first byte
// is 0 (if: when the condition fails, skip to the op's else (op, 1) or end
// (op, 2)), 1 (else: skip to the end) or 2 (end). EmScript_NextCmd(em, p)
// steps over the command at p (a switch on every op's length),
// EmScript_SkipBlock(em, p, op, 2) over one command and any nested block of
// the same op, EmScript_SkipToEnd(em, p, op) to past the op's end.
EmScript_Run = 0x00726380; // type:func
EmScript_SkipBlock = 0x00731CA0; // type:func
EmScript_NextCmd = 0x00731DA0; // type:func
EmScript_SkipToEnd = 0x00733150; // type:func
